1→"""Emit user-configured messages at lifecycle phase transitions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json as _json
     6→import logging
     7→import os as _os
     8→import urllib.error as _urlerr
     9→import urllib.request as _urlreq
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    12→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    14→    COARSE_PHASE_MAP,
    15→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    16→    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    17→)
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→# Phases that are NOT postflight — everything else counts as postflight.
    22→_NON_POSTFLIGHT = frozenset({LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN})
    23→
    24→_HERMES_PORT_FILE = _os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
    25→
    26→
    27→def _hermes_available() -> bool:
    28→    """Check if Hermes integration is enabled in config."""
    29→    try:
    30→        config = load_config()
    31→    except (OSError, ValueError):
    32→        return False
    33→    return bool(config.get("hermes_enabled", False))
    34→
    35→
    36→def _hermes_port() -> int:
    37→    try:
    38→        with open(_HERMES_PORT_FILE) as f:
    39→            return int(f.read().strip())
    40→    except (OSError, ValueError):
    41→        return 47823
    42→
    43→
    44→def _hermes_get(path: str) -> dict:
    45→    """GET a Hermes control API endpoint. Stdlib-only, no deps."""
    46→    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{_hermes_port()}{path}"
    47→    req = _urlreq.Request(url, method="GET")
    48→    try:
    49→        with _urlreq.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
    50→            return _json.loads(resp.read())
    51→    except _urlerr.HTTPError as e:
    52→        return _json.loads(e.read())
    53→    except (_urlerr.URLError, OSError) as e:
    54→        return {"error": str(e)}
    55→
    56→
    57→def _hermes_send_message(text: str, mode: str = "queue") -> dict:
    58→    """Send a message/command to the running Hermes agent. Stdlib-only, no deps."""
    59→    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{_hermes_port()}/sessions/_any/message"
    60→    data = _json.dumps({"text": text, "mode": mode}).encode()
    61→    req = _urlreq.Request(url, data=data, method="POST",
    62→                          headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    63→    try:
    64→        with _urlreq.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
    65→            return _json.loads(resp.read())
    66→    except _urlerr.HTTPError as e:
    67→        return _json.loads(e.read())
    68→    except (_urlerr.URLError, OSError) as e:
    69→        return {"error": str(e)}
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_hermes_model(phase: str, hermes_models: dict) -> str | None:
    73→    """Resolve a phase to a 'provider:model' string from hermes_models config.
    74→
    75→    Lookup: exact phase → coarse phase → 'review' (fallback for non-execute).
    76→    Returns None if no model is configured for this phase.
    77→    """
    78→    spec = hermes_models.get(phase)
    79→    if not spec:
    80→        coarse = COARSE_PHASE_MAP.get(phase)
    81→        if coarse:
    82→            spec = hermes_models.get(coarse)
    83→    if not spec and phase not in _NON_POSTFLIGHT:
    84→        spec = hermes_models.get("review")
    85→    return spec or None
    86→
    87→
    88→def _switch_hermes_model(phase: str) -> bool:
    89→    """Switch the running Hermes agent's model based on the phase.
    90→
    91→    Reads model mapping from hermes_models in config.json.
    92→    Returns True if switch was triggered.
    93→    """
    94→    if not _hermes_available():
    95→        return False
    96→
    97→    try:
    98→        config = load_config()
    99→    except (OSError, ValueError):
   100→        return False
   101→
   102→    hermes_models = config.get("hermes_models", {})
   103→    if not hermes_models:
   104→        return False
   105→
   106→    spec = _resolve_hermes_model(phase, hermes_models)
   107→    if not spec:
   108→        return False
   109→
   110→    try:
   111→        result = _hermes_send_message(f"/model {spec}", mode="interrupt")
   112→        if result.get("success"):
   113→            _hermes_send_message("continue", mode="queue")
   114→            print(f"🔄 Hermes model → {spec} (phase: {phase})")
   115→            return True
   116→        else:
   117→            logger.debug("Hermes model switch failed: %s", result.get("error", ""))
   118→            return False
   119→    except Exception as exc:
   120→        logger.debug("Hermes model switch skipped: %s", exc)
   121→        return False
   122→
   123→
   124→_AUTOREPLY_PROMPT = (
   125→    "You are an autonomous code repair agent working through a desloppify queue. "
   126→    "After each task, run the next desloppify command as instructed. "
   127→    "Do not stop or ask for confirmation — keep going until the queue is empty."
   128→)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _ensure_hermes_autoreply() -> None:
   132→    """Enable autoreply on the Hermes session if not already active.
   133→
   134→    Checks the session state via GET /sessions/_any. If autoreply is
   135→    already enabled, does nothing — so it's safe to call on every
   136→    phase transition without clobbering an existing config.
   137→    """
   138→    if not _hermes_available():
   139→        return
   140→    try:
   141→        info = _hermes_get("/sessions/_any")
   142→        if info.get("autoreply", {}).get("enabled"):
   143→            return
   144→        _hermes_send_message(
   145→            f"/autoreply {_AUTOREPLY_PROMPT}",
   146→            mode="queue",
   147→        )
   148→        logger.debug("Hermes autoreply enabled for desloppify session")
   149→    except Exception as exc:
   150→        logger.debug("Hermes autoreply check skipped: %s", exc)
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_transition_message(new_phase: str) -> bool:
   154→    """Print a transition message if one is configured for *new_phase*.
   155→
   156→    Lookup order: exact phase → coarse phase → ``postflight`` (if the
   157→    phase is not execute/scan).
   158→
   159→    Also triggers a Hermes model switch if the control API is available.
   160→
   161→    Returns True if a message was emitted.
   162→    """
   163→    # Ensure autoreply is enabled so the agent keeps working autonomously
   164→    _ensure_hermes_autoreply()
   165→
   166→    # Switch Hermes model for this phase (best-effort, non-blocking)
   167→    _switch_hermes_model(new_phase)
   168→
   169→    try:
   170→        config = load_config()
   171→    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
   172→        logger.debug("transition message skipped (config load): %s", exc)
   173→        return False
   174→
   175→    messages = config.get("transition_messages")
   176→    if not isinstance(messages, dict) or not messages:
   177→        return False
   178→
   179→    # Try exact phase first, then coarse fallback, then postflight.
   180→    text = messages.get(new_phase)
   181→    if text is None:
   182→        coarse = COARSE_PHASE_MAP.get(new_phase)
   183→        if coarse and coarse != new_phase:
   184→            text = messages.get(coarse)
   185→    if text is None and new_phase not in _NON_POSTFLIGHT:
   186→        text = messages.get("postflight")
   187→
   188→    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
   189→        return False
   190→
   191→    clean = text.strip()
   192→    print(f"\n{'─' * 60}")
   193→    print(f"TRANSITION INSTRUCTION — entering {new_phase} phase")
   194→    print(clean)
   195→    print(f"{'─' * 60}")
   196→    print_user_message(f"Hey, did you see the above? Please act on this: {clean}")
   197→    return True
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = ["emit_transition_message"]
   201→
```

    16→
    17→def cmd_dev(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    18→    """Dispatch developer subcommands."""
    19→    action = getattr(args, "dev_action", None)
    20→    if action == "scaffold-lang":
    21→        try:
    22→            _cmd_scaffold_lang(args)
    23→        except ValueError as ex:
    24→            raise CommandError(str(ex)) from ex
    25→        return
    26→    if action == "test-hermes":
    27→        _cmd_test_hermes()
    28→        return
    29→    raise CommandError("Unknown dev action. Use `desloppify dev scaffold-lang`.")
    30→
    31→
    32→def _normalize_lang_name(raw: str) -> str:
    33→    name = raw.strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    34→    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]*", name):
    35→        raise ValueError("language name must match [a-z][a-z0-9_]*")
    36→    if keyword.iskeyword(name):
    37→        raise ValueError(f"language name cannot be a Python keyword: {name}")
    38→    return name
    39→
    40→
    41→def _normalize_extensions(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    42→    out: list[str] = []
    43→    for val in values or []:
    44→        ext = val.strip()
    45→        if not ext:
    46→            continue
    47→        if not ext.startswith("."):
    48→            ext = "." + ext
    49→        if not re.fullmatch(r"\.[a-z0-9]+", ext):
    50→            raise ValueError(f"invalid extension: {val!r}")
    51→        out.append(ext)
    52→    if not out:
    53→        raise ValueError("at least one --extension is required")
    54→    seen: set[str] = set()
    55→    deduped: list[str] = []
    56→    for ext in out:
    57→        if ext not in seen:
    58→            seen.add(ext)
    59→            deduped.append(ext)
    60→    return deduped
    61→
    62→
    63→def _normalize_markers(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    64→    out: list[str] = []
    65→    for val in values or []:
    66→        marker = val.strip()
    67→        if marker and marker not in out:
    68→            out.append(marker)
    69→    return out
    70→
    71→
    72→def _class_name(lang_name: str) -> str:
    73→    return "".join(part.capitalize() for part in lang_name.split("_")) + "Config"
    74→
    75→
    76→def _template_files(
    77→    lang_name: str,
    78→    extensions: list[str],
    79→    markers: list[str],
    80→    default_src: str,
    81→) -> dict[str, str]:
    82→    class_name = _class_name(lang_name)
    83→    return build_scaffold_files(
    84→        lang_name=lang_name,
    85→        class_name=class_name,
    86→        extensions=extensions,
    87→        markers=markers,
    88→        default_src=default_src,
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→def _render_array(items: list[str]) -> str:
    93→    return ", ".join(repr(x) for x in items)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _append_toml_array_item(text: str, key: str, value: str) -> str:
    97→    pattern = re.compile(
    98→        rf"(^\s*{re.escape(key)}\s*=\s*\[)(.*?)(\]\s*$)",
    99→        re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL,
   100→    )
   101→    match = pattern.search(text)
   102→    if not match:
   103→        return text
   104→
   105→    raw = match.group(2).strip()
   106→    parsed = ast.literal_eval("[" + raw + "]") if raw else []
   107→    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
   108→        return text
   109→    if value in parsed:
   110→        return text
   111→
   112→    parsed.append(value)
   113→    replacement = f"{match.group(1)}{_render_array(parsed)}{match.group(3)}"
   114→    return text[: match.start()] + replacement + text[match.end() :]
   115→
   116→
   117→def _wire_pyproject(lang_name: str) -> bool:
   118→    pyproject_path = get_project_root() / "pyproject.toml"
   119→    if not pyproject_path.is_file():
   120→        return False
   121→
   122→    original = pyproject_path.read_text()
   123→    updated = original
   124→    updated = _append_toml_array_item(
   125→        updated,
   126→        "testpaths",
   127→        f"desloppify/languages/{lang_name}/tests",
   128→    )
   129→
   130→    if updated != original:
   131→        safe_write_text(pyproject_path, updated)
   132→        return True
   133→    return False
   134→
   135→
   136→def _cmd_scaffold_lang(args: object) -> None:
   137→    raw_name = getattr(args, "name", "")
   138→    lang_name = _normalize_lang_name(raw_name)
   139→    extensions = _normalize_extensions(getattr(args, "extension", None))
   140→    markers = _normalize_markers(getattr(args, "marker", None))
   141→    default_src = getattr(args, "default_src", "src") or "src"
   142→    force = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   143→    wire_pyproject = bool(getattr(args, "wire_pyproject", True))
   144→
   145→    lang_dir = get_project_root() / "desloppify" / "languages" / lang_name
   146→    if lang_dir.exists() and not force:
   147→        raise CommandError(
   148→            f"Language directory already exists: {lang_dir}. Use --force to overwrite."
   149→        )
   150→
   151→    files = _template_files(lang_name, extensions, markers, default_src)
   152→    for rel_path, content in files.items():
   153→        target = lang_dir / rel_path
   154→        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   155→        if target.exists() and not force:
   156→            continue
   157→        safe_write_text(target, content)
   158→
   159→    wired = _wire_pyproject(lang_name) if wire_pyproject else False
   160→
   161→    print(colorize(f"Scaffolded language plugin: {lang_name}", "green"))
   162→    print(f"  Path: {lang_dir}")
   163→    print(f"  Extensions: {', '.join(extensions)}")
   164→    print(f"  Markers: {', '.join(markers) if markers else '(none)'}")
   165→    print(f"  pyproject.toml updated: {'yes' if wired else 'no'}")
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            "  Next: implement real phases/commands/detectors and run pytest.", "dim"
   169→        )
   170→    )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _cmd_test_hermes() -> None:
   174→    """Test Hermes model switching — switch to a random model and back."""
   175→    import random
   176→    import time
   177→
   178→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.transition_messages import (
   179→        _hermes_available,
   180→        _hermes_get,
   181→        _hermes_send_message,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→    if not _hermes_available():
   185→        print(colorize('Hermes not enabled. Set "hermes_enabled": true in config.json', "yellow"))
   186→        return
   187→
   188→    # Get current model
   189→    info = _hermes_get("/sessions/_any")
   190→    if "error" in info:
   191→        print(colorize(f"Cannot reach Hermes: {info['error']}", "red"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    original_model = info.get("model", "unknown")
   195→    original_provider = info.get("provider", "unknown")
   196→    print(f"  Current model: {original_provider}:{original_model}")
   197→
   198→    # Pick a random test model
   199→    test_models = [
   200→        ("openrouter", "google/gemini-2.5-flash"),
   201→        ("openrouter", "meta-llama/llama-4-scout"),
   202→        ("openrouter", "mistralai/mistral-medium-3"),
   203→    ]
   204→    test_provider, test_model = random.choice(test_models)
   205→
   206→    # Switch to test model
   207→    print(f"  Switching to: {test_provider}:{test_model}")
   208→    result = _hermes_send_message(f"/model {test_provider}:{test_model}", mode="queue")
   209→    if not result.get("success"):
   210→        print(colorize(f"  Switch failed: {result.get('error', '?')}", "red"))
   211→        return
   212→    print(colorize("  ✓ Switch command sent", "green"))
   213→
   214→    # Wait a moment, then switch back
   215→    time.sleep(2)
   216→    print(f"  Switching back to: {original_provider}:{original_model}")
   217→    result = _hermes_send_message(f"/model {original_provider}:{original_model}", mode="queue")
   218→    if not result.get("success"):
   219→        print(colorize(f"  Switch-back failed: {result.get('error', '?')}", "red"))
   220→        return
   221→    print(colorize("  ✓ Restored original model", "green"))
   222→
```

    10→

    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→_NEXT_TASK_INSTRUCTIONS = (
    16→    "A desloppify task was just marked complete. Here's what to do next:\n"
    17→    "\n"
    18→    "1. Run `desloppify next` to see the next task in the queue\n"
    19→    "2. Read and understand the issue — explore the relevant files and scope\n"
    20→    "3. Execute the fix thoroughly and verify it works\n"
    21→    "4. Once you're happy with it, commit and push:\n"
    22→    "   `git add -A && git commit -m '<describe the fix>' && git push`\n"
    23→    "5. Record the commit: `desloppify plan commit-log record`\n"
    24→    "6. Mark it resolved: `desloppify resolve <pattern> --fixed --attest '<what you did>'`"
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _hermes_reset_and_instruct() -> None:
    29→    """Reset Hermes context and inject next-task instructions via control API."""
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.transition_messages import (
    31→        _hermes_available,
    32→        _hermes_send_message,
    33→    )
    34→
    35→    if not _hermes_available():
    36→        return
    37→    try:
    38→        # Reset conversation to clear stale context from the previous task
    39→        result = _hermes_send_message("/reset", mode="interrupt")
    40→        if not result.get("success"):
    41→            return
    42→
    43→        # Queue the next-task instructions
    44→        _hermes_send_message(_NEXT_TASK_INSTRUCTIONS, mode="queue")
    45→    except Exception as exc:
    46→        logger.debug("Hermes next-task injection skipped: %s", exc)
    47→
    48→
    49→def print_no_match_warning(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    50→    status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    51→    print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→
    54→def print_fixed_next_user_message(
    55→    *,
    56→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    57→    plan: dict | None,
    58→    next_command: str,
    59→    mid_cluster: bool,
    60→    cluster_ctx: ClusterContext,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    if args.status != "fixed" or plan is None or next_command != "desloppify next":
    63→        return
    64→
    65→    if mid_cluster:
    66→        print_user_message(
    67→            f"Hey — task done, {cluster_ctx.cluster_remaining} left in"
    68→            f" cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'. Run `desloppify next`"
    69→            " to pick up the next one. Keep going — don't stop until the"
    70→            " cluster is finished."
    71→        )
    72→    elif cluster_ctx.cluster_completed and cluster_ctx.cluster_name:
    73→        print_user_message(
    74→            f"Hey — nice, you just finished cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'."
    75→            " Check the commit tracking above — this is a good time to"
    76→            " commit and push your work. Then run `desloppify next` to"
    77→            " pick up your next task."
    78→        )
    79→    else:
    80→        print_user_message(
    81→            "Hey — nice, on to the next one. Run `desloppify next`"
    82→            " to pick up your next task. Before moving on, glance at the"
    83→            " commit tracking above — if there's a decent chunk of"
    84→            " uncommitted work, consider whether now's a good time"
    85→            " to commit and push. Otherwise just keep going."
    86→        )
    87→
    88→    # Also inject via Hermes control API for a clean context switch
    89→    _hermes_reset_and_instruct()
    90→
    91→
    92→__all__ = ["print_fixed_next_user_message", "print_no_match_warning"]
    93→

1→"""update-skill command: install or update the desloppify skill document."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import urllib.error
     7→import urllib.request
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.skill_docs import (
    10→    SKILL_BEGIN,
    11→    SKILL_END,
    12→    SKILL_TARGETS,
    13→    SKILL_VERSION,
    14→    SKILL_VERSION_RE,
    15→    SkillInstall,
    16→    find_installed_skill,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    19→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    20→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    21→
    22→_RAW_BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peteromallet/desloppify/main/docs"
    23→
    24→
    25→def _download(filename: str) -> str:
    26→    """Download a file from the desloppify docs directory on GitHub."""
    27→    url = f"{_RAW_BASE}/{filename}"
    28→    with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:  # nosec B310
    29→        return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
    30→
    31→
    32→def _build_section(skill_content: str, overlay_content: str | None) -> str:
    33→    """Assemble the complete skill section from downloaded parts."""
    34→    parts = [skill_content.rstrip()]
    35→    if overlay_content:
    36→        parts.append(overlay_content.rstrip())
    37→    return "\n\n".join(parts) + "\n"
    38→
    39→
    40→# Interfaces whose skill systems parse YAML frontmatter and require ``---``
    41→# to appear on the very first line of the file.
    42→_FRONTMATTER_FIRST_INTERFACES = frozenset({"amp", "codex"})
    43→
    44→
    45→def _ensure_frontmatter_first(content: str) -> str:
    46→    """Move YAML frontmatter to the top if HTML comments precede it.
    47→
    48→    Some skill systems (e.g. AMP) require ``---`` on line 1 for frontmatter
    49→    parsing.  SKILL.md ships with ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and a version
    50→    comment before the ``---`` block.  This function relocates those HTML
    51→    comment lines to just after the closing ``---``.
    52→    """
    53→    lines = content.split("\n")
    54→
    55→    # Find the opening ``---``.
    56→    fm_start = None
    57→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    58→        if line.strip() == "---":
    59→            fm_start = i
    60→            break
    61→    if fm_start is None or fm_start == 0:
    62→        return content  # already fine or no frontmatter
    63→
    64→    # Collect the HTML-comment lines that precede the frontmatter.
    65→    prefix_lines = lines[:fm_start]
    66→
    67→    # Find the closing ``---``.
    68→    fm_end = None
    69→    for i, line in enumerate(lines[fm_start + 1 :], fm_start + 1):
    70→        if line.strip() == "---":
    71→            fm_end = i
    72→            break
    73→    if fm_end is None:
    74→        return content  # malformed frontmatter, leave untouched
    75→
    76→    # Reassemble: frontmatter first, then the prefix lines, then the rest.
    77→    reordered = (
    78→        lines[fm_start : fm_end + 1]
    79→        + prefix_lines
    80→        + lines[fm_end + 1 :]
    81→    )
    82→    return "\n".join(reordered)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _replace_section(file_content: str, new_section: str) -> str:
    86→    """Replace the desloppify section in a shared file, preserving surrounding content.
    87→
    88→    Uses first ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and last ``<!-- desloppify-end -->``
    89→    so the overlay (which also has an end marker) is captured correctly.
    90→    """
    91→    begin = file_content.find(SKILL_BEGIN)
    92→    end = file_content.rfind(SKILL_END)
    93→    if begin == -1 or end == -1:
    94→        # No section markers — append (first install into existing shared file).
    95→        return file_content.rstrip() + "\n\n" + new_section
    96→
    97→    before = file_content[:begin]
    98→    after = file_content[end + len(SKILL_END):]
    99→    before = before.rstrip() + "\n\n" if before.strip() else ""
   100→    after = "\n" + after.lstrip("\n") if after.strip() else "\n"
   101→    return before + new_section + after
   102→
   103→
   104→def resolve_interface(
   105→    explicit: str | None = None,
   106→    install: SkillInstall | None = None,
   107→) -> str | None:
   108→    """Resolve which interface to update.
   109→
   110→    Uses the explicit argument if given, otherwise infers from an existing
   111→    install's overlay marker or file path.
   112→    """
   113→    if explicit:
   114→        return explicit.lower()
   115→
   116→    if install is None:
   117→        install = find_installed_skill()
   118→    if not install:
   119→        return None
   120→
   121→    if install.overlay:
   122→        return install.overlay.lower()
   123→
   124→    for name, (target, _overlay, _ded) in SKILL_TARGETS.items():
   125→        if target == install.rel_path:
   126→            return name
   127→    return None
   128→
   129→
   130→def _update_installed_skill_with_deps(
   131→    interface: str,
   132→    *,
   133→    download_fn,
   134→    get_project_root_fn,
   135→    safe_write_text_fn,
   136→    colorize_fn,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface."""
   139→    target_rel, overlay_name, dedicated = SKILL_TARGETS[interface]
   140→    target_path = get_project_root_fn() / target_rel
   141→
   142→    print(colorize_fn(f"Downloading skill document ({interface})...", "dim"))
   143→    try:
   144→        skill_content = download_fn("SKILL.md")
   145→        overlay_content = download_fn(f"{overlay_name}.md") if overlay_name else None
   146→    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as exc:
   147→        print(colorize_fn(f"Download failed: {exc}", "red"))
   148→        return False
   149→
   150→    if "desloppify-skill-version" not in skill_content:
   151→        print(colorize_fn("Downloaded content doesn't look like a skill document.", "red"))
   152→        return False
   153→
   154→    new_section = _build_section(skill_content, overlay_content)
   155→    if interface in _FRONTMATTER_FIRST_INTERFACES:
   156→        new_section = _ensure_frontmatter_first(new_section)
   157→
   158→    target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   159→    if dedicated:
   160→        result = new_section
   161→    elif target_path.is_file():
   162→        existing = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   163→        result = _replace_section(existing, new_section)
   164→    else:
   165→        result = new_section
   166→
   167→    safe_write_text_fn(target_path, result)
   168→
   169→    version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(new_section)
   170→    version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "?"
   171→    print(
   172→        colorize_fn(
   173→            f"Updated {target_rel} (v{version}, tool expects v{SKILL_VERSION})",
   174→            "green",
   175→        )
   176→    )
   177→    return True
   178→
   179→
   180→def update_installed_skill(interface: str) -> bool:
   181→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface.
   182→
   183→    Returns True on success, False on failure. Prints status messages.
   184→    """
   185→    return _update_installed_skill_with_deps(
   186→        interface,
   187→        download_fn=_download,
   188→        get_project_root_fn=get_project_root,
   189→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   190→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   191→    )
   192→
   193→
   194→def _run_cmd_update_skill(
   195→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   196→    *,
   197→    resolve_interface_fn,
   198→    update_installed_skill_fn,
   199→    colorize_fn,
   200→) -> None:
   201→    """Run the update-skill command with injectable package seams."""
   202→    interface = resolve_interface_fn(getattr(args, "interface", None))
   203→
   204→    if not interface:
   205→        print(colorize_fn("No installed skill document found.", "yellow"))
   206→        print()
   207→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   208→        print(f"Install with: desloppify update-skill <{names}>")
   209→        return
   210→
   211→    if interface not in SKILL_TARGETS:
   212→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   213→        print(colorize_fn(f"Unknown interface '{interface}'.", "red"))
   214→        print(f"Available: {names}")
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    update_installed_skill_fn(interface)
   218→
   219→
   220→def cmd_update_skill(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   221→    """Install or update the desloppify skill document."""
   222→    _run_cmd_update_skill(
   223→        args,
   224→        resolve_interface_fn=resolve_interface,
   225→        update_installed_skill_fn=update_installed_skill,
   226→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   227→    )
   228→

   123→
   124→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
   125→    """Return a config dict with all keys set to their defaults."""
   126→    return {k: copy.deepcopy(v.default) for k, v in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   127→
   128→
   129→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
   130→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
   131→    try:
   132→        parsed = int(value)
   133→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   134→        return MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, False
   135→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   136→    return parsed, valid
   137→
   138→
   139→def coerce_target_score(
   140→    value: object, *, fallback: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   141→) -> float:
   142→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
   143→    if is_numeric(fallback):
   144→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
   145→    else:
   146→        fallback_value = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   147→
   148→    if is_numeric(value):
   149→        parsed = float(value)
   150→    elif isinstance(value, str):
   151→        text = value.strip()
   152→        if not text:
   153→            parsed = fallback_value
   154→        else:
   155→            try:
   156→                parsed = float(text)
   157→            except ValueError:
   158→                parsed = fallback_value
   159→    else:
   160→        parsed = fallback_value
   161→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
   162→
   163→
   164→def target_strict_score_from_config(
   165→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   166→) -> float:
   167→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
   168→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   169→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
   170→    else:
   171→        raw = fallback
   172→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = [
   176→    "CONFIG_SCHEMA",
   177→    "DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE",
   178→    "MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE",
   179→    "MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE",
   180→    "ConfigKey",
   181→    "_coerce_target_strict_score",
   182→    "coerce_target_score",
   183→    "default_config",
   184→    "target_strict_score_from_config",
   185→]

## Summary

I have completed a thorough search of the `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify` directory for Hermes control API usage. Here are my findings:

### Files Using Hermes Control API

**Primary Implementation Files:**

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/transition_messages.py`**
   - Core Hermes control API integration module
   - Implements HTTP calls to the Hermes control API on `127.0.0.1:47823` (or port from `~/.hermes/control_api.port`)
   - Key functions:
     - `_hermes_available()` - Checks if Hermes is enabled in config
     - `_hermes_port()` - Reads port from `~/.hermes/control_api.port` file (defaults to 47823)
     - `_hermes_get(path)` - Sends GET requests to control API endpoints
     - `_hermes_send_message(text, mode)` - Sends messages/commands via POST to `/sessions/_any/message` endpoint
     - `_switch_hermes_model(phase)` - Switches running Hermes model using `/model` command
     - `_ensure_hermes_autoreply()` - Enables autoreply via `/autoreply` command
   - Uses `urllib.request` and `urllib.error` (stdlib only, no external deps)
   - Sends commands: `/model <provider:model>`, `continue`, `/autoreply <prompt>`, `/reset`

2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/messages.py`**
   - Calls `_hermes_reset_and_instruct()` function
   - Imports and uses: `_hermes_available()`, `_hermes_send_message()`
   - Sends `/reset` command (mode: interrupt) and injects next-task instructions via control API
   - Located at lines 28-46

3. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/dev.py`**
   - Contains `_cmd_test_hermes()` function (lines 173-222)
   - Imports: `_hermes_available()`, `_hermes_get()`, `_hermes_send_message()`
   - Tests model switching by:
     - Getting current model via `GET /sessions/_any`
     - Sending `/model` commands to switch models
     - Verifying success/failure responses

**Configuration & Schema Files:**

4. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/base/config/schema.py`**
   - Defines config schema with two Hermes settings:
     - `hermes_enabled` (bool, default False) - Enables Hermes agent integration
     - `hermes_models` (dict) - Phase → provider:model mapping for model switching
   - Default models: `execute: openrouter:x-ai/grok-4.20-beta`, `review: openrouter:google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview`

5. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/config.json`**

   - Part of skill versioning system

### Control API Endpoints Used

- **GET `/sessions/_any`** - Get current session state (model, provider, autoreply status)
- **POST `/sessions/_any/message`** - Send messages/commands to the running Hermes agent
- Commands sent: `/model`, `/reset`, `/autoreply`, `continue`

### Port Configuration

- **Default port:** 47823 (hardcoded fallback)
- **Port file:** `~/.hermes/control_api.port` (checked first, allows runtime override)

No usage of `gateway.hermes_control_client` or direct Python imports from the Hermes gateway module was found. All integration uses stdlib HTTP calls only.
